/bin/zsh -lc "nl -ba v3/src/definition/schema/vocabulary.ts | sed -n '1,420p'" in /Users/felho/dev/pairflow
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	/**
     2	 * ADR-019 (accepted 2026-08-05): the declaration VOCABULARY, as types.
     3	 *
     4	 * This module is TYPES ONLY — it defines the shape a surface declaration
     5	 * may take, and nothing else. The declaration itself is data
     6	 * (`templateFormat.ts`, D4's canonical home); the machines that consume
     7	 * it are `engine.ts` (the validator) and `normalizer.ts` (D3's second,
     8	 * separately-named capability).
     9	 *
    10	 * Every construct below is one of ADR-019 §2.2's admitted vocabulary
    11	 * entries, each of which passed the audit's ≥2-independent-ratified-rows
    12	 * test (`v3/implementation/schema-expressiveness-audit.md` §2.2, standing
    13	 * sha256 `bfed8210…`). Adding a construct is a D7 amendment of the ADR,
    14	 * never a silent edit here.
    15	 */
    16	
    17	/**
    18	 * A message template: literal text with `{placeholder}` slots the engine
    19	 * interpolates (vocabulary #18 — `message:`, the PARITY carrier; ADR-019
    20	 * D5 pre-names message wording as a delta class, and a literal template
    21	 * is the choice that keeps the measured wording byte-identical).
    22	 *
    23	 * The closed placeholder set, all supplied by the engine from the node's
    24	 * own evaluation context — never rule-specific:
    25	 *
    26	 * - `{path}`      the finding's own path
    27	 * - `{key}`       the offending key, raw
    28	 * - `{keyJson}`   the offending key, JSON-quoted
    29	 * - `{value}`     the offending value, DESCRIBED (`a map`/`a list`/literal)
    30	 * - `{valueRaw}`  the offending value, rendered bare (no quoting) — the
    31	 *                 form the measured messages that embed a value inside
    32	 *                 their own quotes need
    33	 * - `{valueJson}` the offending value, JSON-quoted
    34	 * - `{source}`    the offending value's raw source slice, JSON-quoted
    35	 * - `{grammar}`   the grammar's regular-expression source
    36	 * - `{members}`   the enum/allowlist members, comma-joined
    37	 * - `{keys}`      the node's legal keyset, comma-joined
    38	 * - `{label}`     the referencing site's label for a value class
    39	 * - `{ownerKey}`  the key of the nearest enclosing OPEN-map entry
    40	 */
    41	export type MessageTemplate = string;
    42	
    43	/**
    44	 * A path into the value graph, in the declaration's own address form:
    45	 * `$` root, dotted segments, `*` for "every entry of an open map", and a
    46	 * leading `..` for one level up from the citing node.
    47	 */
    48	export type DeclPath = string;
    49	
    50	/** vocabulary #12 — the selector roots. `injected` is D8's ADMITTED
    51	 * widening: a selector root that is an injected SET rather than a
    52	 * document node (a set is a set; the relation and finding form are
    53	 * unchanged). */
    54	export type Selector =
    55	  | { readonly keysOf: DeclPath }
    56	  | { readonly valuesOf: DeclPath }
    57	  | { readonly collect: DeclPath }
    58	  | { readonly union: readonly Selector[] }
    59	  | { readonly injected: "gateCatalog" };
    60	
    61	/**
    62	 * vocabulary #12 — a membership relation between the citing node's value
    63	 * (or its keys) and a selector's set.
    64	 *
    65	 * `at` is the finding's PATH GRAIN and exists because the measured lanes
    66	 * DISAGREE (audit §5 F3): the catalog-resolution lane reports at the
    67	 * BINDING, not at `…uses`. `code` is vocabulary #17.
    68	 */
    69	export interface MembershipRule {
    70	  readonly relation: "memberOf" | "keysSubsetOf" | "disjointFrom";
    71	  readonly target: Selector;
    72	  /** `self` = the citing node's own path (the default); `container` = its
    73	   * parent's path. */
    74	  readonly at?: "self" | "container";
    75	  readonly code?: string;
    76	  readonly message: MessageTemplate;
    77	  /** vocabulary #13 — suppression beyond the implicit container rule. */
    78	  readonly dependsOn?: readonly string[];
    79	}
    80	
    81	/** vocabulary #11 — the duplicate lane and its path grain. */
    82	export interface UniqueRule {
    83	  readonly grain: "perOccurrence";
    84	  readonly at: "index" | "container";
    85	  readonly message: MessageTemplate;
    86	}
    87	
    88	/**
    89	 * The keyset lane ORDER. It is declared, not hard-coded, for the same
    90	 * reason `at:` is (audit §5 F3): the measured nodes DISAGREE, and an
    91	 * engine that picks one silently moves findings. Three measured orders:
    92	 *
    93	 * - `missingThenUnknown`             — the root keyset
    94	 * - `unknownThenPerKey`              — ref, round, activation, the
    95	 *                                      runtimeContext spec, every gate
    96	 *                                      config (the DEFAULT)
    97	 * - `unknownThenMissingThenValues`   — the step keyset
    98	 */
    99	export type LaneOrder =
   100	  | "missingThenUnknown"
   101	  | "unknownThenPerKey"
   102	  | "unknownThenMissingThenValues";
   103	
   104	/** Every node carries its declaration TAG (the contract's pointer target,
   105	 * D4) and the ratified rows it realizes (the tag-closure check's other
   106	 * half). `channel` marks a channel-SCOPED node: `file` for a
   107	 * SOURCE-BEARING attribute set (the engine runs it where a source exists
   108	 * and skips it where none does), `direct` for a key that exists only on
   109	 * the direct-construction channel because it belongs to the ADMITTED form
   110	 * rather than the authored one. This is D1's structural channel symmetry
   111	 * — the retired hand-partitioned "realization split". */
   112	interface NodeBase {
   113	  readonly tag: string;
   114	  readonly rows: readonly string[];
   115	  readonly channel?: "both" | "file" | "direct";
   116	  /** vocabulary #9 — materialized ONCE at admission. Plain `default:` is
   117	   * declarable and stays schema-side (ADR-019 D3); DERIVATION is the
   118	   * normalizer's. */
   119	  readonly default?: unknown;
   120	  /** vocabulary #13 — this node's failure suppresses its dependents. */
   121	  readonly gating?: boolean;
   122	  /**
   123	   * vocabulary #2 — the keyset membership obligation, declared on the
   124	   * FIELD rather than on its container, because the measured lanes
   125	   * disagree on both grains: the template's fixed maps report a missing
   126	   * key at the CONTAINER with one shared wording, every delegated config
   127	   * schema reports it at the KEY with its own wording, and one node (a
   128	   * gate binding's `uses`) folds absence into its own type lane.
   129	   */
   130	  readonly presence?: {
   131	    readonly required: true;
   132	    readonly at?: "container" | "self";
   133	    readonly message?: MessageTemplate;
   134	    readonly code?: string;
   135	    /** Absence is reported by this node's OWN type lane, not by a
   136	     * separate missing lane (a gate binding's `uses`). */
   137	    readonly foldedIntoTypeLane?: true;
   138	  };
   139	}
   140	
   141	/** vocabulary #1 `map.fixed` — a closed keyset. The legal keyset IS
   142	 * `Object.keys(fields)`; each field declares its own `presence`. */
   143	export interface MapFixedDecl extends NodeBase {
   144	  readonly kind: "map.fixed";
   145	  readonly containerMessage: MessageTemplate;
   146	  /** The default missing-key wording for fields whose `presence` states
   147	   * none. */
   148	  readonly missingMessage?: MessageTemplate;
   149	  readonly unknownMessage: MessageTemplate;
   150	  readonly laneOrder?: LaneOrder;
   151	  readonly fields: Readonly<Record<string, NodeDecl>>;
   152	  /** vocabulary #10 — fail-loud key removal with migration text. */
   153	  readonly removedKeys?: Readonly<Record<string, MessageTemplate>>;
   154	}
   155	
   156	/** vocabulary #1 `map.open` — an open key class. */
   157	export interface MapOpenDecl extends NodeBase {
   158	  readonly kind: "map.open";
   159	  readonly containerMessage: MessageTemplate;
   160	  /** vocabulary #3 on a map. `gating` because the measured lanes
   161	   * disagree: an EMPTY `steps` map suppresses every rule that selects
   162	   * over its keys, an empty `terminal` list does not. */
   163	  readonly nonempty?: { readonly message: MessageTemplate; readonly gating?: boolean };
   164	  /** vocabulary #5 — the open-map key class and the finding's path grain. */
   165	  readonly keyClass?: ValueClassRefDecl;
   166	  readonly keyLaneAt: "container" | "segment";
   167	  /** The gates subtree's file-channel key-STRINGNESS scan (ch11 walk /
   168	   * GP2): a DEEP scan reporting at the nearest addressable path. */
   169	  readonly deepKeyStringness?: {
   170	    readonly message: MessageTemplate;
   171	    readonly channel: "file";
   172	  };
   173	  readonly keysSubsetOf?: MembershipRule;
   174	  readonly entry: NodeDecl;
   175	}
   176	
   177	/** vocabulary #1 `list`. */
   178	export interface ListDecl extends NodeBase {
   179	  readonly kind: "list";
   180	  readonly containerMessage: MessageTemplate;
   181	  readonly nonempty?: { readonly message: MessageTemplate; readonly gating?: boolean };
   182	  readonly member: NodeDecl;
   183	  /** The member lane's path grain — `container` reports every member at
   184	   * the list's own path (ch8-C17's measured grain). */
   185	  readonly memberLaneAt: "container" | "index";
   186	  readonly unique?: UniqueRule;
   187	  readonly disjointFrom?: MembershipRule;
   188	  readonly memberOf?: MembershipRule;
   189	}
   190	
   191	/** vocabulary #1 `string` (+ #3 `nonempty`, #4 `grammar`). */
   192	export interface StringDecl extends NodeBase {
   193	  readonly kind: "string";
   194	  /** The non-string lane's message. When a node states only `grammar`,
   195	   * the grammar message covers both lanes (ch8-C8's measured form). */
   196	  readonly typeMessage?: MessageTemplate;
   197	  readonly nonempty?: { readonly message: MessageTemplate };
   198	  readonly grammar?: { readonly re: string; readonly message: MessageTemplate };
   199	  readonly memberOf?: MembershipRule;
   200	}
   201	
   202	/** vocabulary #6/#7 — the raw-source ladder and the value-side belt. */
   203	export interface IntegerDecl extends NodeBase {
   204	  readonly kind: "integer";
   205	  /** The alias-free / anchor-free / tag-free / `^[1-9][0-9]*$` ladder over
   206	   * the SOURCE node. File-scoped by construction: no operand exists on the
   207	   * direct-construction channel. */
   208	  readonly sourceForm?: "plainDecimalInteger";
   209	  readonly resolvedForm?: {
   210	    readonly safeInteger: true;
   211	    readonly min: number;
   212	    readonly message: MessageTemplate;
   213	  };
   214	}
   215	
   216	/** vocabulary #8 — an allowlist. `store` is the authored↔stored token map
   217	 * (ch12-C1). */
   218	export interface EnumDecl extends NodeBase {
   219	  readonly kind: "enum";
   220	  /** A member may be channel-SCOPED: the same `channel` attribute the node
   221	   * grain carries, applied at MEMBER grain. It is a widening, not a new
   222	   * construct — the measured token domain genuinely differs by channel
   223	   * (ch12-C1: the FILE face is authored camelCase, the direct face is the
   224	   * stored token the domain type declares). */
   225	  readonly members: readonly {
   226	    readonly value: unknown;
   227	    readonly store?: string;
   228	    readonly channel?: "both" | "file" | "direct";
   229	  }[];
   230	  readonly message: MessageTemplate;
   231	  readonly code?: string;
   232	}
   233	
   234	/** vocabulary #1 `union` (+ #10 `removed` at VALUE grain). */
   235	export interface UnionDecl extends NodeBase {
   236	  readonly kind: "union";
   237	  readonly literals?: readonly string[];
   238	  readonly mapCase?: NodeDecl;
   239	  readonly removedValues?: Readonly<Record<string, MessageTemplate>>;
   240	  readonly message: MessageTemplate;
   241	}
   242	
   243	/** vocabulary #19 — uninterpreted pass-through. */
   244	export interface RawDecl extends NodeBase {
   245	  readonly kind: "raw";
   246	  /** A raw node may still assert a container kind (ch12-C3's `config`). */
   247	  readonly containerMessage?: MessageTemplate;
   248	}
   249	
   250	/** `map.plain + canonicalJsonSafe` — the agent-config value class
   251	 * (ch12-C7). A PLAIN string-keyed map (never a class instance, never a
   252	 * JS Map); a non-plain container SUPPRESSES the canonical lane. */
   253	export interface MapPlainDecl extends NodeBase {
   254	  readonly kind: "map.plain";
   255	  readonly containerMessage: MessageTemplate;
   256	  readonly canonicalJsonSafe: { readonly message: MessageTemplate };
   257	}
   258	
   259	/** vocabulary #15 — a reusable named value class, with the citing site's
   260	 * `{label}`. */
   261	export interface ValueClassRefDecl extends NodeBase {
   262	  readonly kind: "valueClass";
   263	  readonly valueClass: string;
   264	  readonly label?: string;
   265	}
   266	
   267	/** vocabulary #16 — hand-off to an injected registration's own
   268	 * declaration. */
   269	export interface DelegateDecl extends NodeBase {
   270	  readonly kind: "delegate";
   271	  readonly registry: "gateCatalog";
   272	  /** The sibling field whose value names the registration. */
   273	  readonly by: string;
   274	  /** The delegation contract's own belt: a registration that reports
   275	   * failure with ZERO findings must still block admission, or the binding
   276	   * would be admitted with no effective config. A property of the
   277	   * `delegate` construct, not of any one row (audit flag I3). */
   278	  readonly beltMessage: MessageTemplate;
   279	  /** vocabulary #13 — the resolution lane this hand-off presupposes. */
   280	  readonly dependsOn?: readonly string[];
   281	}
   282	
   283	export type NodeDecl =
   284	  | MapFixedDecl
   285	  | MapOpenDecl
   286	  | MapPlainDecl
   287	  | ListDecl
   288	  | StringDecl
   289	  | IntegerDecl
   290	  | EnumDecl
   291	  | UnionDecl
   292	  | RawDecl
   293	  | ValueClassRefDecl
   294	  | DelegateDecl;
   295	
   296	/**
   297	 * vocabulary #12's `equals` relation — a two-direction set equality with
   298	 * its own message per direction and its own path grain per direction
   299	 * (ch8-C16's measured form: the used-but-undeclared finding reports at
   300	 * the container, the declared-but-unused finding at the entry).
   301	 */
   302	export interface EqualsRuleDecl {
   303	  readonly tag: string;
   304	  readonly rows: readonly string[];
   305	  readonly relation: "equals";
   306	  readonly left: Selector;
   307	  readonly right: Selector;
   308	  readonly missingFromLeft: { readonly at: DeclPath; readonly message: MessageTemplate };
   309	  readonly missingFromRight: { readonly at: DeclPath; readonly message: MessageTemplate };
   310	  readonly dependsOn?: readonly string[];
   311	}
   312	
   313	/**
   314	 * D3 — the NORMALIZER's declared hooks. A declaration says what is legal;
   315	 * it does not compute a value, so derivation is named here and realized in
   316	 * `normalizer.ts`, never in the validator. Each hook is a named capability
   317	 * with its ratified rows; adding one is D7 format growth, not an edit.
   318	 */
   319	export type NormalizerHookDecl =
   320	  | {
   321	      readonly tag: string;
   322	      readonly rows: readonly string[];
   323	      readonly hook: "expandAdvancesRound";
   324	      /** The open map of entries the flag map is expanded per. */
   325	      readonly over: DeclPath;
   326	      /** The entry's edge map, relative to the entry. */
   327	      readonly edges: string;
   328	      /** The declared advancing set. */
   329	      readonly advanceSet: DeclPath;
   330	      /** The produced sibling field. */
   331	      readonly into: string;
   332	    }
   333	  | {
   334	      readonly tag: string;
   335	      readonly rows: readonly string[];
   336	      readonly hook: "materializeEffectiveConfigs";
   337	      /** The open map of pipelines whose members carry a delegated config. */
   338	      readonly over: DeclPath;
   339	      /** The binding fields carried into the admitted form verbatim. */
   340	      readonly carry: readonly string[];
   341	      /** The produced field the effective config lands in. */
   342	      readonly into: string;
   343	    };
   344	
   345	/**
   346	 * The SUBSTRATE block (audit §2.3): how bytes become a value graph. Its
   347	 * obligations are declared here as data; the yaml library realizes them,
   348	 * which is why their WORDING is residual R6 (library-owned) rather than
   349	 * engine parity.
   350	 */
   351	export interface SubstrateDecl {
   352	  /** ch8-C6: the read stage's decode wording. */
   353	  readonly read: { readonly message: MessageTemplate };
   354	  readonly parse: {
   355	    /** ch8-C34: the only accepted `%YAML` directive version, and the
   356	     * finding a different one produces (`{key}` is the authored one). */
   357	    readonly directive: { readonly only: string; readonly message: MessageTemplate };
   358	    /** ch8-C4: the duplicate-key wording. */
   359	    readonly duplicateKeyMessage: MessageTemplate;
   360	  };
   361	  /** ch8-C5: the resolved graph must be acyclic, and the finding it
   362	   * yields at the cycle's path. */
   363	  readonly resolve: { readonly graph: { readonly message: MessageTemplate } };
   364	  /** ch8-C23: the CLOSED issue-code namespace. Every `code` any node in
   365	   * the surface declares must be a member — asserted, not assumed. */
   366	  readonly codes: readonly string[];
   367	  /** ch8-C22/C36: the every-stage belt's own finding. */
   368	  readonly internalFailure: { readonly path: string; readonly message: MessageTemplate };
   369	}
   370	
   371	/** One declared surface: substrate + node tree + the named residual's
   372	 * declarable halves. */
   373	export interface SurfaceDecl {
   374	  readonly substrate: SubstrateDecl;
   375	  readonly root: NodeDecl;
   376	  readonly valueClasses: Readonly<Record<string, NodeDecl>>;
   377	  readonly crossRules: readonly EqualsRuleDecl[];
   378	  readonly normalizers: readonly NormalizerHookDecl[];
   379	}

+import { expect, it } from "vitest";
+import { loadTemplate } from "./load.js";
+import { renderMessage } from "./schema/engine.js";
+import { templateFormat } from "./schema/templateFormat.js";
+
+const bytes = (text: string): Uint8Array => new TextEncoder().encode(text);
+
+it("preserves the four moved substrate messages exactly", () => {
+  const read = loadTemplate(new Uint8Array([0xff]));
+  expect(read.ok ? null : read.error.findings[0]?.message).toBe(
+    "invalid UTF-8 byte sequence (templates are strict UTF-8)",
+  );
+  const directive = loadTemplate(bytes("%YAML 1.1\n---\na: 1\n"));
+  expect(directive.ok ? null : directive.error.findings[0]?.message).toBe(
+    "%YAML 1.1 directive: only YAML 1.2 is supported",
+  );
+  const duplicate = loadTemplate(bytes(`ref:
+  id: t
+  version: 1
+start: s
+steps:
+  s:
+    role: r
+    instruction: i
+    transitions: {}
+    agentConfig:
+      seed: &key [a, b]
+      ? [a, b]
+      : first
+      ? *key
+      : second
+terminal:
+  - done
+roles:
+  r: {}
+`));
+  expect(duplicate.ok ? [] : duplicate.error.findings.map((finding) => finding.message)).toContain(
+    "Map keys must be unique",
+  );
+  expect(
+    renderMessage(templateFormat.substrate.internalFailure.message, { value: "boom" }),
+  ).toBe("internal validator failure: boom");
+});

### F3 — A `MapOpenDecl.nonempty.gating` dokumentációja a `steps` tényleges szemantikájának ellenkezőjét állítja

CLASS: IN-SCOPE — A Lens E kifejezetten a deklarációs attribútum doc commentje és az engine-fogyasztás közti, round 1-ben nem jelentett eltéréseket kéri.

WHAT: A vocabulary szerint az üres `steps` map elnyom minden, a kulcsaira szelektáló szabályt, miközben a deklaráció szándékosan nem állít `gating: true`-t, az engine pedig csak explicit `true` esetén teszi a nonempty hibát gatinggé.

WHERE: `v3/src/definition/schema/vocabulary.ts:160`

    17	/**
    18	 * ADR-019 D1: THE ENGINE — one validator over declaration DATA, running
    19	 * the SAME declaration on the file-walk and direct-construction channels.
    20	 *
    21	 * The engine consumes a `SurfaceDecl`; it knows the VOCABULARY and knows
    22	 * nothing about any particular rule. A per-rule branch here (a literal
    23	 * `if (rule === X)`) is ADR-019 D9 tripwire #2 — a STOP and a scope
    24	 * review, never a work-around. Where two measured lanes disagree on a
    25	 * grain (path address, lane order, gating), the disagreement is DECLARED
    26	 * (`at:`, `laneOrder:`, `gating:`), which is the same instrument the
    27	 * audit's §5 F3 identified for path grain.
    28	 *
    29	 * Channel symmetry is structural, not argued (D1): attributes that read
    30	 * SOURCE text (`sourceForm`, the deep key-stringness scan) are declared
    31	 * once with `channel: "file"`; the engine runs them where a source exists
    32	 * and skips them where none does.
    33	 *
    34	 * This module VALIDATES and applies plain `default:` materialization. It
    35	 * does NOT derive values — derivation is the separately-named normalizer
    36	 * (D3, `normalizer.ts`).
    37	 */
    38	
    39	/** The channel the declaration is being run on. */
    40	export type EngineChannel =
    41	  | { readonly kind: "file"; readonly doc: Document; readonly source: string }
    42	  | { readonly kind: "direct" };

    45	  readonly channel: EngineChannel;

    65	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    66	// Value access: ONE accessor over both channels' container forms.
    67	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    68	
    69	type AnyMap = ReadonlyMap<unknown, unknown> | Record<string, unknown>;
    70	
    71	function isResolvedMap(value: unknown): value is ReadonlyMap<unknown, unknown> {
    72	  return value instanceof Map;
    73	}
    74	
    75	/** A container map on EITHER channel: the file channel's `mapAsMap` JS Map
    76	 * or the direct channel's plain object. Arrays are never maps. */
    77	function isContainer(value: unknown): value is AnyMap {
    78	  return typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
    79	}
    80	
    81	function entriesOf(value: AnyMap): readonly (readonly [unknown, unknown])[] {
    82	  if (isResolvedMap(value)) return [...value.entries()];
    83	  return Object.keys(value).map((key) => [key, ownGet(value, key)] as const);
    84	}
    85	
    86	function ownGet(record: Record<string, unknown>, key: string): unknown {
    87	  return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(record, key) ? record[key] : undefined;
    88	}
    89	
    90	function containerHas(value: AnyMap, key: string): boolean {
    91	  if (isResolvedMap(value)) return value.has(key);
    92	  return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(value, key);
    93	}
    94	
    95	function containerGet(value: AnyMap, key: string): unknown {
    96	  if (isResolvedMap(value)) return value.get(key);
    97	  return ownGet(value, key);
    98	}
    99	
   100	/** A PLAIN string-keyed map — a plain object or null-prototype object,
   101	 * never an array, a JS Map, or a class instance (`map.plain`). */
   102	function isPlainMap(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
   103	  if (typeof value !== "object" || value === null || Array.isArray(value)) return false;
   104	  const proto: unknown = Object.getPrototypeOf(value);
   105	  return proto === Object.prototype || proto === null;
   106	}
   107	
   108	/** Own-property WRITE: a step id or event type legally admits `__proto__`;
   109	 * a bracket assignment would set the prototype and drop the own key. */
   110	function defineOwn<T>(target: Record<string, T>, key: string, value: T): void {
   111	  Object.defineProperty(target, key, { configurable: true, enumerable: true, value, writable: true });
   112	}
   113	
   114	/** Realize a resolved value graph as own-property records where every map
   115	 * key is a string, preserving aliased identity through one memo. A map
   116	 * with any non-string key stays a JS Map (its identity class is what the
   117	 * key lanes report on). */
   118	function materialize(value: unknown, seen: WeakMap<object, unknown>): unknown {
   119	  if (typeof value !== "object" || value === null) return value;
   120	  const existing = seen.get(value);
   121	  if (existing !== undefined) return existing;
   122	  if (Array.isArray(value)) {
   123	    const result: unknown[] = [];
   124	    seen.set(value, result);
   125	    for (const item of value) result.push(materialize(item, seen));
   126	    return result;
   127	  }
   128	  if (isResolvedMap(value)) {
   129	    const entries = [...value.entries()];
   130	    if (entries.every(([key]) => typeof key === "string")) {
   131	      const record: Record<string, unknown> = {};
   132	      seen.set(value, record);
   133	      for (const [key, item] of entries) defineOwn(record, key as string, materialize(item, seen));
   134	      return record;
   135	    }
   136	    const map = new Map<unknown, unknown>();
   137	    seen.set(value, map);
   138	    for (const [key, item] of entries) map.set(materialize(key, seen), materialize(item, seen));
   139	    return map;
   140	  }
   141	  return value;
   142	}

   144	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   145	// Paths and message interpolation.
   146	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   147	
   148	/** `$` is the template root; `""` is a delegated config's own root. Both
   149	 * are addressed by their children's bare key (the measured path grammar). */
   150	function joinPath(parent: string, key: string): string {
   151	  return parent === "$" || parent === "" ? key : `${parent}.${key}`;
   152	}
   153	
   154	function indexPath(parent: string, index: number): string {
   155	  return `${parent}[${String(index)}]`;
   156	}
   157	
   158	/** The finding-message value rendering: objects are DESCRIBED, never
   159	 * serialized (a cyclic graph reaches the walk). */
   160	function describeValue(value: unknown): string {
   161	  if (typeof value === "object" && value !== null) {
   162	    return Array.isArray(value) ? "a list" : "a map";
   163	  }
   164	  return JSON.stringify(value) ?? String(value);
   165	}
   166	
   167	/** A key rendered for a message: a string key verbatim, anything else
   168	 * through the same description ladder values use (a non-string key is a
   169	 * real possibility on the file channel). */
   170	function describeKey(key: unknown): string {
   171	  return typeof key === "string" ? key : describeValue(key);
   172	}

   227	// The run state.

   230	interface Frame {
   231	  /** The declaration path of the node being evaluated (`$.steps.*.role`). */
   232	  readonly decl: DeclPath;
   233	  /** The value path the findings address (`steps.review.role`). */
   234	  readonly path: string;
   235	  readonly value: unknown;
   236	  /** The enclosing container's value — the operand of a `..` selector. */
   237	  readonly parentValue: unknown;
   238	  /** The key of the nearest enclosing OPEN-map entry. */
   239	  readonly ownerKey?: string | undefined;

   242	
   243	/** What a node evaluation reports upward: whether the node produced a
   244	 * value its dependents may rely on (the implicit container precondition
   245	 * plus every DECLARED `gating`). */
   246	interface NodeResult {
   247	  readonly ok: boolean;
   248	  readonly value?: unknown;
   249	}
   250	
   251	/** A membership lane whose operand has NOT been evaluated yet. The engine
   252	 * defers it to the end of the walk rather than reading an operand whose
   253	 * reliability is still unknown — a GENERAL property of the `memberOf`
   254	 * construct, not a per-rule placement. */
   255	interface DeferredCheck {
   256	  readonly frame: Frame;
   257	  readonly rule: MembershipRule;
   258	  readonly candidate: unknown;
   259	  readonly slots: Slots;
   260	}
   261	
   262	class Run {
   263	  readonly findings: ValidationFinding[] = [];
   264	  readonly effectiveConfigs = new Map<string, unknown>();
   265	  readonly runtimeContextBindings: string[] = [];
   266	  /** Declaration path / tag → whether every instance produced a usable
   267	   * value. A rule naming an unreliable operand is suppressed. */
   268	  readonly declOk = new Map<string, boolean>();
   269	  /** Declaration paths whose evaluation has completed. An operand that is
   270	   * neither reliable nor unreliable but PENDING defers its rule. */
   271	  readonly completed = new Set<DeclPath>();
   272	  readonly deferred: DeferredCheck[] = [];
   273	  readonly memo = new WeakMap<object, unknown>();
   274	  root: unknown;
   275	
   276	  constructor(
   277	    readonly channel: EngineChannel,
   278	    readonly registries: Readonly<Record<string, GateCatalog | undefined>>,
   279	    readonly valueClasses: Readonly<Record<string, NodeDecl>>,
   280	  ) {}
   281	
   282	  /** The injected set a selector root or a delegation names. */
   283	  registry(name: string): GateCatalog | undefined {
   284	    return this.registries[name];
   285	  }
   286	
   287	  emit(path: string, message: string, code?: string): void {
   288	    this.findings.push(code === undefined ? { path, message } : { path, message, code });
   289	  }
   290	
   291	  mark(key: string, ok: boolean): void {
   292	    if (this.declOk.get(key) === false) return;
   293	    this.declOk.set(key, ok);
   294	  }
   295	
   296	  reliable(key: string): boolean {
   297	    return this.declOk.get(key) !== false;
   298	  }
   299	}
   300	
   301	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   302	// Selectors.
   303	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   304	
   305	type SelectorResult =
   306	  | { readonly status: "ok"; readonly values: readonly string[] }
   307	  | { readonly status: "unreliable" | "pending" };
   308	
   309	const UNRELIABLE = { status: "unreliable" } as const;
   310	const PENDING = { status: "pending" } as const;
   311	
   312	/** Walk a declaration path over the value graph, expanding `*` over open
   313	 * maps. `..` resolves one level up from the citing frame. A prefix that
   314	 * has not been evaluated yet answers PENDING, not "unreliable". */
   315	function resolvePath(
   316	  run: Run,
   317	  from: Frame,
   318	  path: DeclPath,
   319	): { readonly status: "ok"; readonly nodes: unknown[] } | { readonly status: "unreliable" | "pending" } {
   320	  const segments = path.split(".");
   321	  let nodes: unknown[];
   322	  let declPrefix: string;
   323	  const head = segments.shift();
   324	  if (head === "$") {
   325	    nodes = [run.root];
   326	    declPrefix = "$";
   327	  } else if (head === "^") {
   328	    // Parent-relative: the citing node's own container.
   329	    nodes = [from.parentValue];
   330	    declPrefix = from.decl.split(".").slice(0, -1).join(".");
   331	  } else {
   332	    return UNRELIABLE;
   333	  }
   334	  for (const segment of segments) {
   335	    declPrefix = `${declPrefix}.${segment}`;
   336	    if (!run.reliable(declPrefix)) return UNRELIABLE;
   337	    if (!run.completed.has(declPrefix)) return PENDING;
   338	    const next: unknown[] = [];
   339	    for (const node of nodes) {
   340	      if (!isContainer(node)) return UNRELIABLE;
   341	      if (segment === "*") {
   342	        for (const [key, child] of entriesOf(node)) {
   343	          if (typeof key === "string") next.push(child);
   344	        }
   345	      } else {
   346	        if (!containerHas(node, segment)) return UNRELIABLE;
   347	        next.push(containerGet(node, segment));
   348	      }
   349	    }
   350	    nodes = next;
   351	  }
   352	  return { status: "ok", nodes };
   353	}
   354	
   355	function evaluateSelector(run: Run, from: Frame, selector: Selector): SelectorResult {
   356	  if ("injected" in selector) {
   357	    // D8's ADMITTED widening: the selector root is an INJECTED set. A
   358	    // registry resolves rather than enumerates, so membership is asked of
   359	    // it directly (`isMember`); the set itself is never listed.
   360	    return run.registry(selector.injected) === undefined ? UNRELIABLE : { status: "ok", values: [] };
   361	  }
   362	  if ("union" in selector) {
   363	    const parts = selector.union.map((part) => evaluateSelector(run, from, part));
   364	    if (parts.some((part) => part.status === "pending")) return PENDING;
   365	    if (parts.some((part) => part.status === "unreliable")) return UNRELIABLE;
   366	    return {
   367	      status: "ok",
   368	      values: parts.flatMap((part) => (part.status === "ok" ? part.values : [])),
   369	    };
   370	  }
   371	  const target = "keysOf" in selector ? selector.keysOf : "valuesOf" in selector ? selector.valuesOf : selector.collect;
   372	  const resolved = resolvePath(run, from, target);
   373	  if (resolved.status !== "ok") return resolved;
   374	  const values: string[] = [];
   375	  for (const node of resolved.nodes) {
   376	    if ("keysOf" in selector) {
   377	      if (!isContainer(node)) return UNRELIABLE;
   378	      for (const [key] of entriesOf(node)) if (typeof key === "string") values.push(key);
   379	    } else if ("valuesOf" in selector) {
   380	      if (!Array.isArray(node)) return UNRELIABLE;
   381	      for (const item of node) if (typeof item === "string") values.push(item);
   382	    } else {
   383	      if (typeof node !== "string") return UNRELIABLE;
   384	      values.push(node);
   385	    }
   386	  }
   387	  return { status: "ok", values };
   388	}
   389	
   390	/** `memberOf` against an INJECTED root asks the registry; against a
   391	 * document root it asks the collected set. */
   392	function isMember(run: Run, selector: Selector, set: SelectorResult, candidate: string): boolean {
   393	  if ("injected" in selector) return run.registry(selector.injected)?.resolve(candidate) != null;
   394	  return set.status === "ok" && set.values.includes(candidate);
   395	}
   396	
   397	function checkMembership(
   398	  run: Run,
   399	  frame: Frame,
   400	  rule: MembershipRule,
   401	  candidate: unknown,
   402	  slots: Slots,
   403	  local?: ReadonlyMap<string, boolean>,
   404	): void {
   405	  if (rule.dependsOn?.some((tag) => local?.get(tag) === false || !run.reliable(tag)) === true) return;
   406	  const set = evaluateSelector(run, frame, rule.target);
   407	  if (set.status === "pending") {
   408	    run.deferred.push({ frame, rule, candidate, slots });
   409	    return;
   410	  }
   411	  if (set.status === "unreliable") return;
   412	  const at = rule.at === "container" ? parentPath(frame.path) : frame.path;
   413	  const hit = typeof candidate === "string" && isMember(run, rule.target, set, candidate);
   414	  const violated = rule.relation === "disjointFrom" ? hit : !hit;
   415	  if (violated) {
   416	    run.emit(at, render(rule.message, { ...slots, path: at, value: candidate }), rule.code);
   417	  }
   418	}
   419	
   420	function parentPath(path: string): string {
   421	  const bracket = path.lastIndexOf("[");
   422	  const dot = path.lastIndexOf(".");
   423	  if (bracket > dot) return path.slice(0, bracket);
   424	  if (dot < 0) return "$";
   425	  return path.slice(0, dot);
   426	}
   427	
   428	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   429	// The source-form ladder (vocabulary #6) — file channel only.
   430	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   431	
   432	const PLAIN_DECIMAL = /^[1-9][0-9]*$/;
   433	
   434	/**
   435	 * The `plainDecimalInteger` ladder over a YAML node: alias-free,
   436	 * scalar, anchor-free, tag-free, and a `^[1-9][0-9]*$` raw slice, then
   437	 * the resolved safe-integer belt. Its six messages are FIXED by the
   438	 * ladder (they are identical at every measured user), so they live with
   439	 * the construct rather than being restated per node.
   440	 */
   441	const SOURCE_LADDER = {
   442	  alias: "{path} must not be an alias (an alias hides the source form, probe P21)",
   443	  nonScalar: "{path} must be a plain decimal integer scalar",
   444	  anchor: "{path} must not carry an anchor (the anchor token sits outside the range slice)",
   445	  tag: "{path} must not carry a tag (probe P22: a tag can flip the resolved type silently)",
   446	  form: "{path} must be written as a plain decimal integer >= 1; got source form {source}",
   447	  resolved: "{path} must resolve to a safe integer >= 1",
   448	} as const;
   449	
   450	export function sourceLadderFinding(node: unknown, source: string, path: string): string | undefined {
   451	  if (node === undefined || node === null) return undefined;
   452	  if (isAlias(node)) return render(SOURCE_LADDER.alias, { path });
   453	  if (!isScalar(node)) return render(SOURCE_LADDER.nonScalar, { path });
   454	  if (node.anchor !== undefined) return render(SOURCE_LADDER.anchor, { path });
   455	  if (node.tag !== undefined) return render(SOURCE_LADDER.tag, { path });
   456	  const range = node.range;
   457	  const slice = range ? source.slice(range[0], range[1]) : "";
   458	  if (!PLAIN_DECIMAL.test(slice)) {
   459	    return render(SOURCE_LADDER.form, { path, source: JSON.stringify(slice) });
   460	  }
   461	  const resolved: unknown = node.value;
   462	  if (typeof resolved !== "number" || !Number.isSafeInteger(resolved) || resolved < 1) {
   463	    return render(SOURCE_LADDER.resolved, { path });
   464	  }
   465	  return undefined;
   466	}
   467	
   468	/** The YAML-AST address of a value path, for the source-bearing lanes. */
   469	export function astPath(path: string): (string | number)[] {
   470	  const segments: (string | number)[] = [];
   471	  for (const part of path.split(".")) {
   472	    let rest = part;
   473	    const bracket = rest.indexOf("[");
   474	    if (bracket >= 0) {
   475	      const head = rest.slice(0, bracket);
   476	      if (head !== "") segments.push(head);
   477	      rest = rest.slice(bracket);
   478	      for (const match of rest.matchAll(/\[(\d+)\]/gu)) {
   479	        segments.push(Number(match[1]));
   480	      }
   481	      continue;
   482	    }
   483	    segments.push(rest);
   484	  }
   485	  return segments;
   486	}
   487	
   488	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   489	// The acyclic-graph lane (the substrate's `resolve.graph`).
   490	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   490	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   491	
   492	/** DFS with a PATH-SCOPED ancestor set: shared subtrees (legal anchor
   493	 * reuse) are not cycles; only a back-edge is. */
   494	function findCycle(value: unknown, path: string, ancestors: Set<object>): string | undefined {
   495	  if (typeof value !== "object" || value === null) return undefined;
   496	  if (ancestors.has(value)) return path;
   497	  ancestors.add(value);
   498	  try {
   499	    if (Array.isArray(value)) {
   500	      for (let i = 0; i < value.length; i += 1) {
   501	        const hit = findCycle(value[i], joinPath(path, String(i)), ancestors);
   502	        if (hit !== undefined) return hit;
   503	      }
   504	      return undefined;
   505	    }
   506	    if (isResolvedMap(value)) {
   507	      for (const [key, child] of value.entries()) {
   508	        const segment = typeof key === "string" ? key : "<map-key>";
   509	        const keyHit = findCycle(key, joinPath(path, `${segment}<key>`), ancestors);
   510	        if (keyHit !== undefined) return keyHit;
   511	        const valueHit = findCycle(child, joinPath(path, segment), ancestors);
   512	        if (valueHit !== undefined) return valueHit;
   513	      }
   514	    }
   515	    return undefined;
   516	  } finally {
   517	    ancestors.delete(value);
   518	  }
   519	}
   520	
   521	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   522	// Node evaluation.
   523	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   524	
   525	const DEFAULT_MISSING = 'missing required key "{key}"';
   526	
   527	/** Sibling reliability inside ONE fixed-map evaluation. `dependsOn` reads
   528	 * it before the global map so a defect in binding #1 cannot suppress a
   529	 * lane in binding #2. */
   530	type LocalOk = Map<string, boolean>;
   531	
   532	function baseSlots(frame: Frame): Slots {
   533	  return { path: frame.path, value: frame.value, ownerKey: frame.ownerKey, label: frame.label };
   534	}
   535	
   536	function evaluateNode(run: Run, decl: NodeDecl, frame: Frame, local?: LocalOk): NodeResult {
   537	  const result = dispatch(run, decl, frame, local);
   538	  run.completed.add(frame.decl);
   539	  run.mark(frame.decl, result.ok);
   540	  run.mark(decl.tag, result.ok);
   541	  return result;
   542	}
   543	
   544	function dispatch(run: Run, decl: NodeDecl, frame: Frame, local?: LocalOk): NodeResult {
   545	  switch (decl.kind) {
   546	    case "map.fixed":
   547	      return evalMapFixed(run, decl, frame);
   548	    case "map.open":
   549	      return evalMapOpen(run, decl, frame);
   550	    case "map.plain":
   551	      return evalMapPlain(run, decl, frame);
   552	    case "list":
   553	      return evalList(run, decl, frame, local);
   554	    case "string":
   555	      return evalString(run, decl, frame, local);
   556	    case "integer":
   557	      return evalInteger(run, decl, frame);
   558	    case "enum":
   559	      return evalEnum(run, decl, frame);
   560	    case "union":
   561	      return evalUnion(run, decl, frame);
   562	    case "raw":
   563	      return evalRaw(run, decl, frame);
   564	    case "valueClass":
   565	      return evalValueClassRef(run, decl, frame, local);
   566	    case "delegate":
   567	      return evalDelegate(run, decl, frame, local);
   568	  }
   569	}
   570	
   571	function evalValueClassRef(
   572	  run: Run,
   573	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "valueClass" }>,
   574	  frame: Frame,
   575	  local?: LocalOk,
   576	): NodeResult {
   577	  const target = run.valueClasses[decl.valueClass];
   578	  if (target === undefined) return { ok: true, value: frame.value };
   579	  const merged: Frame = { ...frame, label: decl.label ?? frame.label };
   580	  const result = dispatch(run, target, merged, local);
   581	  // The REFERENCING site's gating decides, not the shared class's.
   582	  if (!result.ok && decl.gating === true) run.mark(frame.decl, false);
   583	  return result;
   584	}
   585	
   586	function evalMapFixed(
   587	  run: Run,
   588	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "map.fixed" }>,
   589	  frame: Frame,
   590	): NodeResult {
   591	  const value = frame.value;
   592	  const slots = baseSlots(frame);
   593	  if (!isContainer(value)) {
   594	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.containerMessage, slots));
   595	    return { ok: false };
   596	  }
   597	  const container = value;
   598	  const fieldNames = Object.keys(decl.fields);
   599	  // A field declared for ONE channel is legal only there — the same
   600	  // channel scoping D1 applies to source-bearing attributes, applied to
   601	  // keyset membership.
   602	  const legal = new Set(
   603	    fieldNames.filter((name) => {
   604	      const channel = decl.fields[name]?.channel;
   605	      return channel === undefined || channel === "both" || channel === run.channel.kind;
   606	    }),
   607	  );
   608	  const normalized: Record<string, unknown> = {};
   609	  const local: LocalOk = new Map();
   610	
   611	  const emitUnknownKeys = (): void => {
   612	    for (const [key] of entriesOf(container)) {
   613	      if (typeof key !== "string") {
   614	        run.emit(
   615	          frame.path,
   616	          render(decl.unknownMessage, { ...slots, key, value: key, keys: fieldNames.join(", ") }),
   617	        );
   618	        continue;
   619	      }
   620	      if (legal.has(key)) continue;
   621	      const at = joinPath(frame.path, key);
   622	      const removed = decl.removedKeys?.[key];
   623	      const keySlots: Slots = { ...slots, path: at, key, value: key, keys: fieldNames.join(", ") };
   624	      run.emit(at, render(removed ?? decl.unknownMessage, keySlots));
   625	    }
   626	  };
   627	
   628	  const emitMissing = (name: string): void => {
   629	    const field = decl.fields[name];
   630	    const presence = field?.presence;
   631	    if (field === undefined || presence?.required !== true) return;
   632	    if (containerHas(container, name)) return;
   633	    if (presence.foldedIntoTypeLane === true) {
   634	      // Absence is this node's OWN type lane (a binding's `uses`).
   635	      evaluateNode(run, field, childFrame(frame, name, undefined, container), local);
   636	      local.set(field.tag, false);
   637	      return;
   638	    }
   639	    const at = presence.at === "self" ? joinPath(frame.path, name) : frame.path;
   640	    run.emit(
   641	      at,
   642	      render(presence.message ?? decl.missingMessage ?? DEFAULT_MISSING, { ...slots, path: at, key: name }),
   643	      presence.code,
   644	    );
   645	    local.set(field.tag, false);
   646	  };
   647	
   648	  const evalOneField = (name: string): void => {
   649	    const field = decl.fields[name];
   650	    if (field === undefined) return;
   651	    if (!containerHas(container, name)) {
   652	      // An ABSENT field is where a declared `default:` materializes — the
   653	      // schema-side half of ADR-019 D3.
   654	      if (field.default !== undefined) defineOwn(normalized, name, field.default);
   655	      return;
   656	    }
   657	    const child = childFrame(frame, name, containerGet(container, name), container);
   658	    const result = evaluateNode(run, field, child, local);
   659	    local.set(field.tag, result.ok);
   660	    if (result.ok && result.value !== undefined) defineOwn(normalized, name, result.value);
   661	  };
   662	
   663	  const order = decl.laneOrder ?? "unknownThenPerKey";
   664	  if (order === "missingThenUnknown") {
   665	    for (const name of fieldNames) emitMissing(name);
   666	    emitUnknownKeys();
   667	    for (const name of fieldNames) evalOneField(name);
   668	  } else if (order === "unknownThenMissingThenValues") {
   669	    emitUnknownKeys();
   670	    for (const name of fieldNames) emitMissing(name);
   671	    for (const name of fieldNames) evalOneField(name);
   672	  } else {
   673	    emitUnknownKeys();
   674	    for (const name of fieldNames) {
   675	      if (!containerHas(container, name)) {
   676	        emitMissing(name);
   677	        evalOneField(name);
   678	        continue;
   679	      }
   680	      evalOneField(name);
   681	    }
   682	  }
   683	  return { ok: true, value: normalized };
   684	}
   685	
   686	function childFrame(parent: Frame, key: string, value: unknown, parentValue: unknown): Frame {
   687	  return {
   688	    decl: `${parent.decl}.${key}`,
   689	    path: joinPath(parent.path, key),
   690	    value,
   691	    parentValue,
   692	    ownerKey: parent.ownerKey,
   693	  };
   694	}
   695	
   696	function evalMapOpen(
   697	  run: Run,
   698	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "map.open" }>,
   699	  frame: Frame,
   700	): NodeResult {
   701	  const value = frame.value;
   702	  const slots = baseSlots(frame);
   703	  if (!isContainer(value)) {
   704	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.containerMessage, slots));
   704	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.containerMessage, slots));
   705	    return { ok: false };
   706	  }
   707	  const container = value;
   708	  const entries = entriesOf(container);
   709	  let ok = true;
   710	  if (decl.nonempty !== undefined && entries.length === 0) {
   711	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.nonempty.message, slots));
   712	    if (decl.nonempty.gating === true) ok = false;
   713	  }
   714	  if (decl.deepKeyStringness !== undefined && run.channel.kind === "file") {
   715	    scanKeyStringness(run, container, frame.path, decl.deepKeyStringness.message, new WeakSet());
   716	  }
   717	  const normalized: Record<string, unknown> = {};
   718	  for (const [key, child] of entries) {
   719	    const keyIsString = typeof key === "string";
   720	    const entryPath = keyIsString ? joinPath(frame.path, key) : frame.path;
   721	    if (decl.keyClass !== undefined) {
   722	      const keyFrame: Frame = {
   723	        decl: `${frame.decl}<key>`,
   724	        path: decl.keyLaneAt === "container" ? frame.path : entryPath,
   725	        value: key,
   726	        parentValue: container,
   727	        ownerKey: frame.ownerKey,
   728	      };
   729	      const keyResult = evaluateNode(run, decl.keyClass, keyFrame);
   730	      if (!keyResult.ok && decl.keyClass.gating === true) ok = false;
   731	    }
   732	    if (decl.keysSubsetOf !== undefined) {
   733	      const before = run.findings.length;
   734	      // The rule is the OPEN MAP's, so a `^`-relative selector resolves
   735	      // from the map's own container — never from the map itself.
   736	      const subsetFrame: Frame = {
   737	        decl: frame.decl,
   738	        path: entryPath,
   739	        value: key,
   740	        parentValue: frame.parentValue,
   741	        ownerKey: frame.ownerKey,
   742	      };
   743	      checkMembership(run, subsetFrame, decl.keysSubsetOf, key, {
   744	        ...slots,
   745	        path: entryPath,
   746	        key,
   747	        ownerKey: frame.ownerKey,
   748	      });
   749	      // A key outside the subset is DEAD config: nothing below it has an
   750	      // operand to be validated against (the measured lane's CONTINUE).
   751	      if (run.findings.length > before) continue;
   752	    }
   753	    const entryFrame: Frame = {
   754	      decl: `${frame.decl}.*`,
   755	      path: entryPath,
   756	      value: child,
   757	      parentValue: container,
   758	      ownerKey: keyIsString ? key : frame.ownerKey,
   759	    };
   760	    const entryResult = evaluateNode(run, decl.entry, entryFrame);
   761	    if (keyIsString && entryResult.ok && entryResult.value !== undefined) {
   762	      defineOwn(normalized, key, entryResult.value);
   763	    }
   764	  }
   765	  run.completed.add(`${frame.decl}.*`);
   766	  return { ok, value: normalized };
   767	}
   768	
   769	/** The gates subtree's file-channel key-STRINGNESS scan: a non-string key
   770	 * cannot become an own property and would blind every own-key scan, so it
   771	 * reports at the NEAREST addressable path (the containing map's). */
   772	function scanKeyStringness(
   773	  run: Run,
   774	  value: unknown,
   775	  path: string,
   776	  message: MessageTemplate,
   777	  seen: WeakSet<object>,
   778	): void {
   779	  if (typeof value !== "object" || value === null) return;
   780	  if (seen.has(value)) return;
   781	  seen.add(value);
   782	  if (Array.isArray(value)) {
   783	    value.forEach((item, index) => scanKeyStringness(run, item, indexPath(path, index), message, seen));
   784	    return;
   785	  }
   786	  if (!isResolvedMap(value)) return;
   787	  for (const [key, child] of value.entries()) {
   788	    if (typeof key !== "string") {
   789	      run.emit(path, render(message, { path, key, value: key }));
   790	      continue;
   791	    }
   792	    scanKeyStringness(run, child, joinPath(path, key), message, seen);
   793	  }
   794	}
   795	
   796	function evalList(
   797	  run: Run,
   798	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "list" }>,
   799	  frame: Frame,
   800	  local?: LocalOk,
   801	): NodeResult {
   802	  const value = frame.value;
   803	  const slots = baseSlots(frame);
   804	  if (!Array.isArray(value)) {
   805	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.containerMessage, slots));
   806	    return { ok: false };
   807	  }
   808	  let ok = true;
   809	  if (decl.nonempty !== undefined && value.length === 0) {
   810	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.nonempty.message, slots));
   811	    if (decl.nonempty.gating === true) ok = false;
   812	  }
   813	  const normalized: unknown[] = [];
   814	  const seen = new Set<unknown>();
   815	  const clean: { readonly member: unknown; readonly path: string }[] = [];
   816	  value.forEach((member, index) => {
   817	    const memberPath = decl.memberLaneAt === "container" ? frame.path : indexPath(frame.path, index);
   818	    const memberFrame: Frame = {
   819	      decl: `${frame.decl}[]`,
   820	      path: memberPath,
   821	      value: member,
   822	      parentValue: value,
   823	      ownerKey: frame.ownerKey,
   824	    };
   825	    const before = run.findings.length;
   826	    const result = evaluateNode(run, decl.member, memberFrame, local);
   827	    if (run.findings.length > before) return;
   828	    if (result.value !== undefined) normalized.push(result.value);
   829	    if (decl.memberOf !== undefined) {
   830	      checkMembership(run, memberFrame, decl.memberOf, member, { ...slots, path: memberPath, value: member });
   831	    }
   832	    if (decl.unique?.grain === "perOccurrence") {
   833	      const at = decl.unique.at === "container" ? frame.path : memberPath;
   834	      if (seen.has(member)) {
   835	        run.emit(at, render(decl.unique.message, { ...slots, path: at, value: member }));
   836	      }
   837	      seen.add(member);
   838	    }
   839	    clean.push({ member, path: memberPath });
   840	  });
   841	  if (decl.disjointFrom !== undefined) {
   842	    for (const entry of clean) {
   843	      const memberFrame: Frame = {
   844	        decl: `${frame.decl}[]`,
   845	        path: entry.path,
   846	        value: entry.member,
   847	        parentValue: value,
   848	        ownerKey: frame.ownerKey,
   849	      };
   850	      checkMembership(run, memberFrame, decl.disjointFrom, entry.member, {
   851	        ...slots,
   852	        path: entry.path,
   853	        value: entry.member,
   854	      });
   855	    }
   856	  }
   857	  run.completed.add(`${frame.decl}[]`);
   858	  return { ok, value: normalized };
   859	}
   860	
   861	function evalString(
   862	  run: Run,
   863	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "string" }>,
   864	  frame: Frame,
   865	  local?: LocalOk,
   866	): NodeResult {
   867	  const value = frame.value;
   868	  const slots: Slots = { ...baseSlots(frame), grammar: decl.grammar?.re };
   869	  if (typeof value !== "string") {
   870	    // A node that declares NO type lane leaves the non-string case to its
   871	    // membership lane, which emits ONE finding for both faults (the
   872	    // measured form of `start` and of a transition target).
   873	    if (decl.typeMessage !== undefined) {
   874	      run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.typeMessage, slots), decl.presence?.code);
   875	      return { ok: false };
   876	    }
   877	  } else {
   878	    if (decl.nonempty !== undefined && value.length === 0) {
   879	      run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.nonempty.message, slots), decl.presence?.code);
   880	      return { ok: false };
   881	    }
   882	    if (decl.grammar !== undefined && !new RegExp(decl.grammar.re, "u").test(value)) {
   883	      run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.grammar.message, slots));
   884	      return { ok: false };
   885	    }
   886	  }
   887	  if (decl.memberOf !== undefined) {
   888	    const before = run.findings.length;
   889	    checkMembership(run, frame, decl.memberOf, value, slots, local);
   890	    if (run.findings.length > before) return { ok: false, value };
   891	  }
   892	  return typeof value === "string" ? { ok: true, value } : { ok: false };
   893	}
   894	
   895	function evalInteger(
   896	  run: Run,
   897	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "integer" }>,
   898	  frame: Frame,
   899	): NodeResult {
   900	  const value = frame.value;
   901	  if (decl.sourceForm !== undefined && run.channel.kind === "file") {
   902	    const node: unknown = run.channel.doc.getIn(astPath(frame.path), true);
   903	    const message = sourceLadderFinding(node, run.channel.source, frame.path);
   904	    if (message !== undefined) {
   905	      run.emit(frame.path, message);
   906	      return { ok: false };
   907	    }
   908	    return { ok: true, value };
   909	  }
   910	  const belt = decl.resolvedForm;
   911	  if (belt?.safeInteger === true) {
   912	    if (typeof value !== "number" || !Number.isSafeInteger(value) || value < belt.min) {
   913	      run.emit(frame.path, render(belt.message, baseSlots(frame)), decl.presence?.code);
   914	      return { ok: false };
   915	    }
   916	  }
   917	  return { ok: true, value };
   918	}
   919	
   920	function evalEnum(
   921	  run: Run,
   922	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "enum" }>,
   923	  frame: Frame,
   924	): NodeResult {
   925	  const value = frame.value;
   926	  const legal = decl.members.filter(
   927	    (one) => one.channel === undefined || one.channel === "both" || one.channel === run.channel.kind,
   928	  );
   929	  const slots: Slots = {
   930	    ...baseSlots(frame),
   931	    members: legal.map((one) => String(one.value)).join(", "),
   932	  };
   933	  const member = legal.find((one) => one.value === value);
   934	  if (member !== undefined) return { ok: true, value: member.store ?? member.value };
   935	  run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.message, slots), decl.code);
   936	  return { ok: false };
   937	}
   938	
   939	function evalUnion(
   940	  run: Run,
   941	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "union" }>,
   942	  frame: Frame,
   943	): NodeResult {
   944	  const value = frame.value;
   945	  const slots = baseSlots(frame);
   946	  if (typeof value === "string") {
   947	    const removed = decl.removedValues?.[value];
   948	    if (removed !== undefined) {
   949	      run.emit(frame.path, render(removed, slots));
   950	      return { ok: false };
   951	    }
   952	    if (decl.literals?.includes(value) === true) return { ok: true, value };
   953	  }
   954	  if (isContainer(value) && decl.mapCase !== undefined) {
   955	    return evaluateNode(run, decl.mapCase, frame);
   956	  }
   957	  run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.message, slots));
   958	  return { ok: false };
   959	}
   960	
   961	function evalRaw(run: Run, decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "raw" }>, frame: Frame): NodeResult {
   962	  if (decl.containerMessage !== undefined && !isContainer(frame.value)) {
   963	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.containerMessage, baseSlots(frame)));
   964	    return { ok: false };
   965	  }
   966	  return { ok: true, value: materialize(frame.value, run.memo) };
   967	}
   968	
   969	function evalMapPlain(
   970	  run: Run,
   971	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "map.plain" }>,
   972	  frame: Frame,
   973	): NodeResult {
   974	  const value = frame.value;
   975	  const slots = baseSlots(frame);
   976	  const realized = materialize(value, run.memo);
   977	  if (!isPlainMap(realized)) {
   978	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.containerMessage, { ...slots, value: realized }));
   979	    return { ok: false };
   980	  }
   981	  if (!isCanonicalizable(realized)) {
   982	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.canonicalJsonSafe.message, slots));
   983	    return { ok: false };
   984	  }
   985	  return { ok: true, value: realized };
   986	}
   987	
   988	function evalDelegate(
   989	  run: Run,
   990	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "delegate" }>,
   991	  frame: Frame,
   992	  local?: LocalOk,
   993	): NodeResult {
   994	  if (decl.dependsOn?.some((tag) => local?.get(tag) === false) === true) return { ok: true };
   995	  const owner = frame.parentValue;
   996	  const by = isContainer(owner) ? containerGet(owner, decl.by) : undefined;
   997	  if (typeof by !== "string") return { ok: true };
   998	  const registration = run.registry(decl.registry)?.resolve(by) ?? null;
   999	  if (registration === null) return { ok: true };
  1000	  if (registration.requiresRuntimeContext) run.runtimeContextBindings.push(parentPath(frame.path));
  1001	  const raw = frame.value === undefined ? undefined : materialize(frame.value, run.memo);
  1002	  const result = registration.validateAndNormalizeConfig(raw);
  1003	  if (result.ok) {
  1004	    run.effectiveConfigs.set(parentPath(frame.path), result.effective);
  1005	    return { ok: true, value: result.effective };
  1006	  }
  1007	  if (result.findings.length === 0) {
  1008	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.beltMessage, { ...baseSlots(frame), key: by, value: by }));
  1009	    return { ok: false };
  1010	  }
  1011	  for (const finding of result.findings) {
  1012	    const at = finding.path === "" ? frame.path : `${frame.path}.${finding.path}`;
  1013	    run.emit(at, finding.message, finding.code);
  1014	  }
  1015	  return { ok: false };
  1016	}
  1017	
  1018	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1019	// Cross rules and the deferred queue.
  1020	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1021	
  1022	function unique(values: readonly string[]): string[] {
  1023	  return [...new Set(values)];
  1024	}
  1025	
  1026	/** vocabulary #12's `equals` — two-direction set equality, each direction
  1027	 * carrying its own path grain and wording (ch8-C16's measured form). */
  1028	function evaluateEquals(run: Run, frame: Frame, rule: EqualsRuleDecl): void {
  1029	  if (rule.dependsOn?.some((tag) => !run.reliable(tag)) === true) return;
  1030	  const left = evaluateSelector(run, frame, rule.left);
  1031	  const right = evaluateSelector(run, frame, rule.right);
  1032	  if (left.status !== "ok" || right.status !== "ok") return;
  1033	  const declared = unique(left.values);
  1034	  const used = unique(right.values);
  1035	  for (const value of used) {
  1036	    if (declared.includes(value)) continue;
  1037	    const at = render(rule.missingFromLeft.at, { value });
  1038	    run.emit(at, render(rule.missingFromLeft.message, { path: at, value }));
  1039	  }
  1040	  for (const value of declared) {
  1041	    if (used.includes(value)) continue;
  1042	    const at = render(rule.missingFromRight.at, { value });
  1043	    run.emit(at, render(rule.missingFromRight.message, { path: at, value }));
  1044	  }
  1045	}
  1046	
  1047	function runDeferred(run: Run): void {
  1048	  // A membership lane deferred for a PENDING operand runs once, in the
  1049	  // order the walk queued it. The queue is drained, never re-fed: every
  1050	  // operand has completed by now, so a second pass could only loop.
  1051	  const queued = run.deferred.splice(0, run.deferred.length);
  1052	  for (const check of queued) {
  1053	    checkMembership(run, check.frame, check.rule, check.candidate, check.slots);
  1054	  }
  1055	  run.deferred.length = 0;
  1056	}
  1057	
  1058	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1059	// Entry points.
  1060	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1061	
  1062	/**
  1063	 * Run a surface declaration over a value graph. ONE computation serves
  1064	 * both channels (D1): the caller supplies `channel`, and the source-bearing
  1065	 * attributes are inert where no source exists.
  1066	 */
  1067	export function runSurface(surface: SurfaceDecl, value: unknown, opts: EngineOptions): EngineRun {
  1068	  const run = new Run(opts.channel, { gateCatalog: opts.catalog }, surface.valueClasses);
  1069	  run.root = value;
  1070	  const rootFrame: Frame = { decl: "$", path: "$", value, parentValue: undefined };
  1071	  const rootDecl = surface.root;
  1072	
  1073	  // The root container precondition: with no map operand nothing below has
  1074	  // an address, so the ONE finding is the whole result. It applies where
  1075	  // the ROOT is declared as a map — a surface whose root is any other
  1076	  // kind evaluates through its own node like every other.
  1077	  const rootIsMap =
  1078	    rootDecl.kind === "map.fixed" || rootDecl.kind === "map.open" || rootDecl.kind === "map.plain";
  1079	  if (rootIsMap && !isContainer(value)) {
  1080	    run.emit("$", render(rootDecl.containerMessage, { path: "$", value }));

   468	/** The YAML-AST address of a value path, for the source-bearing lanes. */
   469	export function astPath(path: string): (string | number)[] {
   470	  const segments: (string | number)[] = [];
   471	  for (const part of path.split(".")) {
   472	    let rest = part;
   473	    const bracket = rest.indexOf("[");
   474	    if (bracket >= 0) {
   475	      const head = rest.slice(0, bracket);
   476	      if (head !== "") segments.push(head);
   477	      rest = rest.slice(bracket);
   478	      for (const match of rest.matchAll(/\[(\d+)\]/gu)) {
   479	        segments.push(Number(match[1]));
   480	      }
   481	      continue;
   482	    }
   483	    segments.push(rest);
   484	  }
   485	  return segments;
   486	}
   487	
   488	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   489	// The acyclic-graph lane (the substrate's `resolve.graph`).
   490	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   491	
   492	/** DFS with a PATH-SCOPED ancestor set: shared subtrees (legal anchor
   493	 * reuse) are not cycles; only a back-edge is. */
   494	function findCycle(value: unknown, path: string, ancestors: Set<object>): string | undefined {
   495	  if (typeof value !== "object" || value === null) return undefined;
   496	  if (ancestors.has(value)) return path;
   497	  ancestors.add(value);
   498	  try {
   499	    if (Array.isArray(value)) {
   500	      for (let i = 0; i < value.length; i += 1) {
   501	        const hit = findCycle(value[i], joinPath(path, String(i)), ancestors);
   502	        if (hit !== undefined) return hit;
   503	      }
   504	      return undefined;
   505	    }
   506	    if (isResolvedMap(value)) {
   507	      for (const [key, child] of value.entries()) {
   508	        const segment = typeof key === "string" ? key : "<map-key>";
   509	        const keyHit = findCycle(key, joinPath(path, `${segment}<key>`), ancestors);
   510	        if (keyHit !== undefined) return keyHit;
   511	        const valueHit = findCycle(child, joinPath(path, segment), ancestors);
   512	        if (valueHit !== undefined) return valueHit;
   513	      }
   514	    }
   515	    return undefined;
   516	  } finally {
   517	    ancestors.delete(value);
   518	  }
   519	}

   532	function baseSlots(frame: Frame): Slots {
   533	  return { path: frame.path, value: frame.value, ownerKey: frame.ownerKey, label: frame.label };
   534	}
   535	
   536	function evaluateNode(run: Run, decl: NodeDecl, frame: Frame, local?: LocalOk): NodeResult {
   537	  const result = dispatch(run, decl, frame, local);
   538	  run.completed.add(frame.decl);
   539	  run.mark(frame.decl, result.ok);
   540	  run.mark(decl.tag, result.ok);
   541	  return result;
   542	}
   543	
   544	function dispatch(run: Run, decl: NodeDecl, frame: Frame, local?: LocalOk): NodeResult {
   545	  switch (decl.kind) {
   546	    case "map.fixed":
   547	      return evalMapFixed(run, decl, frame);
   548	    case "map.open":
   549	      return evalMapOpen(run, decl, frame);
   550	    case "map.plain":
   551	      return evalMapPlain(run, decl, frame);
   552	    case "list":
   553	      return evalList(run, decl, frame, local);
   554	    case "string":
   555	      return evalString(run, decl, frame, local);
   556	    case "integer":
   557	      return evalInteger(run, decl, frame);
   558	    case "enum":
   559	      return evalEnum(run, decl, frame);
   560	    case "union":
   561	      return evalUnion(run, decl, frame);
   562	    case "raw":
   563	      return evalRaw(run, decl, frame);
   564	    case "valueClass":
   565	      return evalValueClassRef(run, decl, frame, local);
   566	    case "delegate":
   567	      return evalDelegate(run, decl, frame, local);
   568	  }
   569	}
   570	
   571	function evalValueClassRef(
   572	  run: Run,
   573	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "valueClass" }>,
   574	  frame: Frame,
   575	  local?: LocalOk,
   576	): NodeResult {
   577	  const target = run.valueClasses[decl.valueClass];
   578	  if (target === undefined) return { ok: true, value: frame.value };
   579	  const merged: Frame = { ...frame, label: decl.label ?? frame.label };
   580	  const result = dispatch(run, target, merged, local);
   581	  // The REFERENCING site's gating decides, not the shared class's.
   582	  if (!result.ok && decl.gating === true) run.mark(frame.decl, false);
   583	  return result;
   584	}

   614	        run.emit(

   617	        );
   618	        continue;
   619	      }

codex
### F4 — A `deepKeyStringness.channel` továbbra is deklarált, de nem fogyasztott attribútum

CLASS: IN-SCOPE — Ez ugyanaz a round 1 által megnevezett DECLARED-BUT-NOT-CONSUMED defect class, és a Lens C kifejezetten az egész vocabulary újrasöprését kéri.

WHAT: A vocabulary és a deklaráció `deepKeyStringness.channel: "file"` mezőt hordoz, de az engine nem olvassa a mezőt; a file-csatornát saját `run.channel.kind === "file"` literállal dönti el.

WHERE: `v3/src/definition/schema/vocabulary.ts:171`

EVIDENCE:

WHY IT MATTERS: A deklaráció azt sugallja, hogy a lane csatornája deklarációs bemenet, miközben a tényleges authority az engine hard-coded ága; a mező megváltoztatása nem változtatna viselkedést.

FIX SKETCH: A legkisebb korrekció vagy a redundáns, fix értékű `channel` mező eltávolítása a nested attribútumból és a deklarációból, vagy az engine feltételének ténylegesen ezt a mezőt kell olvasnia; új construct nem szükséges.

  1026	/** vocabulary #12's `equals` — two-direction set equality, each direction
  1027	 * carrying its own path grain and wording (ch8-C16's measured form). */
  1028	function evaluateEquals(run: Run, frame: Frame, rule: EqualsRuleDecl): void {
  1029	  if (rule.dependsOn?.some((tag) => !run.reliable(tag)) === true) return;
  1030	  const left = evaluateSelector(run, frame, rule.left);
  1031	  const right = evaluateSelector(run, frame, rule.right);
  1032	  if (left.status !== "ok" || right.status !== "ok") return;
  1033	  const declared = unique(left.values);
  1034	  const used = unique(right.values);
  1099	  const result = evaluateNode(run, rootDecl, rootFrame);
  1100	  runDeferred(run);
  1101	  for (const rule of surface.crossRules) evaluateEquals(run, rootFrame, rule);
  1102	  return {
  1103	    findings: run.findings,
   118	  }
   119	}
   120	
   121	/**
   122	 * Run every declared normalizer hook over a VALIDATED value. The caller
   123	 * guarantees the value carries no findings — a declaration that refused
   124	 * the input never reaches derivation.
   125	 */
   126	export function normalize(
   127	  surface: SurfaceDecl,
   128	  value: unknown,
   129	  effectiveConfigs: ReadonlyMap<string, unknown>,
   130	): unknown {
   131	  if (!isRecord(value)) return value;
   132	  for (const hook of surface.normalizers) {
   133	    if (hook.hook === "expandAdvancesRound") {
   134	      expandFlagMaps(value, hook);
   135	      continue;
   136	    }
   137	    materializeConfigs(value, hook, effectiveConfigs);
   138	  }
   139	  return value;
   140	}

+import { expect, it } from "vitest";
+import { loadTemplate } from "./load.js";
+import { renderMessage } from "./schema/engine.js";

+

+  const read = loadTemplate(new Uint8Array([0xff]));
+  expect(read.ok ? null : read.error.findings[0]?.message).toBe(
+    "invalid UTF-8 byte sequence (templates are strict UTF-8)",
+  );
+  const directive = loadTemplate(bytes("%YAML 1.1\n---\na: 1\n"));

+    role: r
+    instruction: i

+      seed: &key [a, b]

+      ? *key
+      : second
+terminal:
+  - done

+  r: {}

codex
### F5 — Két további fix értékű deklarációs mező értékét nem olvassa egyik gép sem

CLASS: IN-SCOPE — A `sourceForm` és az equals-rule `relation` ugyanabba a DECLARED-BUT-NOT-CONSUMED osztályba esik, mint a fold által javított `required`, `grain` és `safeInteger`.

WHAT: Az engine a `sourceForm` puszta jelenlétére mindig a plain-decimal laddert futtatja, a `crossRules` minden elemére pedig ellenőrzés nélkül equals-értékelést futtat; a deklarált `"plainDecimalInteger"` és `"equals"` értékeknek nincs olvasója.

WHERE: `v3/src/definition/schema/vocabulary.ts:208`

EVIDENCE:

WHY IT MATTERS: A declaration reader mindkét értéket viselkedést választó discriminánsnak látja, miközben az engine a construct jelenlétéből hard-code-olja a választást; egy későbbi vocabulary-bővítés false-green lehet.

FIX SKETCH: A fold már alkalmazott mintájával az engine olvassa a konkrét `sourceForm` és `relation` értéket; ha a mezők szándékosan csak egyértékű névcímkék, a kisebb korrekció az eltávolításuk.

### F6 — A cross-rule és normalizer hook `rows` hivatkozásait sem runtime, sem hygiene check nem olvassa

CLASS: IN-SCOPE — Ezek deklarált traceability mezők, de a jelenlegi „every declared node” assertion csak a root node-fát járja, ezért a cross-rule és a két normalizer `rows` bájtjai dekoratívak maradnak.

WHAT: A `d-roleset`, `n-advances-round` és `n-effective-config` ratified-row listáit egyik gép sem fogyasztja, a hygiene teszt pedig nem járja a `crossRules` vagy `normalizers` tömböket.

WHERE: `v3/src/definition/schema/templateFormat.ts:512`

```text
   707	      switch (decl.kind) {
```
